Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the bose-einstein object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 542. The moduli space has dimension at most 660 with 4 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_542(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 542. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^660(S, F) is trivial. QED.
